During the registration process, subjects receive a subcutaneous injection ostensibly containing a standard immunological registry marker. In practice, the injection also delivers a proprietary isotopic tracer compound — a stable, non-radioactive rare-earth chelate — that binds to keratinocytes and persists in shed skin cells, hair follicles, and exhaled particulate for 14 to 18 months. Tessera's licensed scanner infrastructure, embedded in door frames, HVAC intake grilles, and transit turnstiles, can detect the unique isotopic ratio of an individual's tag from ambient environmental samples without any direct contact or subject awareness.

Tessera Corponation CogniFold AR-7 Associative Memory Compression Engine#📋

name Tessera Corponation CogniFold AR-7 Associative Memory Compression Engine
brand name Tessera
product name Tessera Corponation CogniFold AR-7 Associative Memory Compression Engine
type technology
aliases
  • The Folder
  • AR-Seven
  • Tessera Stack
  • Zipbrain
subcategory augmentation
description The CogniFold AR-7 is a palm-sized neurocompute implant seated in the temporal-parietal junction region via a minimally invasive keyhole insertion procedure, designed to extend functional human memory capacity by implementing a lossy-to-lossless hierarchical compression architecture modeled on sleep-cycle consolidation processes. The device intercepts memory encoding signals during formation and applies Tessera's proprietary associative tagging algorithm — CogniFold Markup Language, or CML — which cross-references new memories against a personal semantic index and compresses redundant experiential data while flagging high-priority experiences for full-fidelity retention. Users report the subjective experience as memories feeling more 'organized' and 'accessible,' though critics note that CML's prioritization decisions are shaped by Tessera's default relevance weighting, which heavily favors commercially and professionally useful information. The AR-7 also includes a voluntary recall acceleration mode, marketed as 'ClearFocus,' which temporarily suppresses emotional memory coloration to allow faster, more objective information retrieval — a feature heavily adopted by legal professionals, corporate negotiators, and interrogation-adjacent security roles. The device communicates with Tessera's cloud index infrastructure via Saltmarsh Telecom's encrypted neural data band, meaning full functionality requires an active subscription to Tessera's CogniFold Continuity Service.
tier availability Tier 1+
developers
  • TESSERA CORPONATION
  • SALTMARSH TELECOM
  • CINDERBLOCK AI
base technologies
  • Temporal-parietal junction signal interception
  • Associative semantic indexing algorithms
  • Lossy-to-lossless hierarchical memory compression
  • Sleep-cycle neural consolidation modeling
  • Encrypted neural data band transmission
enables
  • Vastly expanded functional working memory capacity
  • Searchable personal memory archive with external query access
  • Emotional dissociation on-demand for high-stakes decision-making
  • Corporate memory audit and compliance verification
  • Cross-user associative memory network research applications
social impact The CogniFold AR-7's subscription model has introduced a new axis of cognitive inequality in Meridian 88 — when a user's Continuity Service lapses, the compressed memory segments become inaccessible rather than deleted, effectively holding years of consolidated memories in escrow until payment resumes, a practice Tessera's legal team has successfully defended three times in the Corporate Arbitration Tribunal as 'temporary service suspension rather than deprivation.' The ClearFocus emotional suppression feature has generated significant controversy after a series of domestic violence cases where defendants argued that chronic ClearFocus use had impaired their capacity to form normal empathic responses, a defense that has been both accepted and rejected by different tribunal panels, creating a legal grey zone that Tessera's lobbyists have shown no interest in resolving.
story hooks

Tessera Corponation StratoTram VX-8 Aerial Cable Transit Gondola#📋

name Tessera Corponation StratoTram VX-8 Aerial Cable Transit Gondola

aliases

subcategory transportation
description The StratoTram VX-8 is Tessera Corponation's modular aerial gondola transit system designed to thread passenger movement through Meridian 88's densest vertical construction zones, where ground-level congestion and subterranean flooding make conventional transit impractical. Each gondola cabin is a self-balancing, climate-controlled capsule carrying up to 22 passengers, suspended from a tensioned carbon-composite cable network anchored to Palladian-certified building spines. The VX-8 distinguishes itself from earlier cable transit generations through its dynamic cable tension management system — onboard actuators in each gondola communicate with anchor pylons to redistribute load in real time, allowing the system to remain operational in Great Lakes wind events up to Category 3 storm conditions without emergency shutdown.

The gondola cabins themselves are fabricated with AeroSkin-derived adaptive paneling that reduces aerodynamic drag during high-wind conditions and doubles as a surface for embedded Saltmarsh Telecom signal relay nodes, making each passing gondola a mobile component of the city's communications mesh. Tessera integrated a tiered access system into the boarding terminals: Tier 1 and Tier 2 citizens board from enclosed climate-controlled stations with biometric fast-lane processing, while lower-tier riders queue at exposed platform decks with no environmental protection. The class stratification is architectural and deliberate.

The VX-8 network currently spans 34 active corridors across the mid-city strata, with Tessera holding exclusive operating licenses on 28 of them. Competing operators have attempted to establish parallel cable routes only to find that Tessera's anchor agreements with Palladian-constructed buildings create effective monopoly chokepoints over the most economically valuable transit corridors.

Tier 4 riders who depend on the VX-8 as their only reliable transit option during flood seasons have documented hypothermia cases from exposed waiting platforms, while Tessera's quarterly reports highlight 'premium ridership satisfaction' metrics. The system's monopoly on anchor rights has made it effectively impossible for municipal or community-operated transit alternatives to emerge in mid-city corridors, cementing Tessera's stranglehold on basic mobility.

The FLFS can cut, drill, and shape any material — including diamond, sapphire, and exotic metamaterials — with tolerances measured in nanometers. The system fabricates components for neural interfaces, quantum computing hardware, and metamaterial structures that cannot be manufactured by any other method.

Tessera Industries Quantum Tunneling Data Storage#📋

name Tessera Industries Quantum Tunneling Data Storage

product name Tessera Industries Quantum Tunneling Data Storage
type technology
aliases

  • Quantum Vault
  • Tunnel Store

subcategory computing
description A data storage system that encodes information in quantum states of electrons held in potential wells, using quantum tunneling effects to read and write data. QTDS achieves storage densities that exceed conventional systems by a factor of 10,000, encoding a petabyte of data in a chip the size of a fingernail. The quantum encoding also provides inherent security — reading the data requires knowledge of the quantum state configuration, and unauthorized measurement collapses the data, destroying it. Information stored in QTDS is either accessible to authorized users or destroyed — there is no middle ground.
tier availability Tier 4+

  • TESSERA INDUSTRIES

  • Petabyte-scale portable storage
  • Physically tamper-proof data containers
social impact QTDS has made information simultaneously more portable and more secure than ever before. An entire corporation's data archive fits on a chip that can be swallowed, and that data will destroy itself if anyone without the quantum configuration key attempts to access it. This has changed the dynamics of espionage — stealing data requires stealing the key as well as the medium, and brute-force approaches to breaking the encryption result in total data loss. QTDS has also enabled new forms of dead drops and information smuggling that are virtually undetectable.
story hooks
  • A QTDS chip containing a complete copy of a corporation's financial records has been found with its quantum configuration key intact — someone had total access to both the data and the key, and they wanted it found.

social impact The PrismWatch DX-7 has transformed Meridian 88's advertising infrastructure into one of the world's most comprehensive passive biometric collection systems, with the legal protection of being classified as commercial rather than surveillance data. Citizens who consciously avoid cameras, block neural signals, and use identity countermeasures typically have no awareness that the advertising panels they walk past are harvesting their retinal signatures. The behavioral and emotional data layer has enabled Vantablack's clients to conduct what analysts describe as 'population mood management' — adjusting advertising content across districts in real time to suppress civic unrest indicators, a capability that has attracted both enormous corporate investment and allegations of mass psychological manipulation.
story hooks
  • A client needs their retinal signature purged from Vantablack's impression database before a corporate tribunal uses it to place them at a location — the only way in is through a Vantablack data center with air-gapped archive servers
  • Vantablack's emotional response data shows a city-wide surge in a specific stress signature concentrated in a particular district — someone is paying to suppress that data, and a journalist wants to know why
  • A fixer discovers that the PrismWatch system in a specific corridor has been modified to flag a particular retinal signature for immediate physical intercept — someone has turned the advertising panels into an assassination trigger
  • The DX-7 database contains retinal scans from inside a corporate boardroom — someone installed a panel in a private space, and the data captured there is worth an enormous amount to the right buyer

Zheng-Dao Bioelectric CellStack BM-3 Biological Microbial Fuel Cell Implant#📋

name Zheng-Dao Bioelectric CellStack BM-3 Biological Microbial Fuel Cell Implant
brand name Zheng
product name Zheng-Dao Bioelectric CellStack BM-3 Biological Microbial Fuel Cell Implant
type technology
aliases
  • Gut Battery
  • BioCell
  • BM-3
  • Fermenter
subcategory energy
description The CellStack BM-3 is a surgically implanted biological microbial fuel cell array designed to harvest electrical energy from the electrochemical activity of engineered gut microbiome colonies. Developed by Zheng-Dao Bioelectric as a long-duration auxiliary power source for high-draw neural augmentations, the BM-3 consists of three titanium-encased fuel cell chambers implanted in the peritoneal space, each seeded with a proprietary strain of Zheng-Dao's GeoElectric-7 exoelectrogenic bacteria. These organisms oxidize metabolic byproducts — acetate, lactate, and short-chain fatty acids — drawn passively from intestinal interstitial fluid, generating a sustained low-amperage direct current output of 15–40 mA at 0.8–1.2 V per chamber. A tri-chamber array produces sufficient power to continuously supplement or replace standard battery packs for mid-tier neural implants, eliminating the need for external recharging in routine use.

The BM-3's key innovation over earlier biofuel cell prototypes is its Symbiotic Interface Layer (SIL) — a semi-permeable hydrogel membrane coating each chamber that allows metabolite exchange while preventing immune recognition of the bacterial colonies. This dramatically reduces the chronic inflammation and fibrous encapsulation that rendered previous designs clinically unreliable within months of implantation. Combined with Novafold's ImmunoVeil TX-4 rejection suppression protocol as a standard co-prescription, the BM-3 achieves a clinically validated operational lifespan of 6–9 years before bacterial colony viability declines and chamber replacement is required. Output degrades predictably with poor nutrition, illness, or significant metabolic disruption — a vulnerability with real-world consequences for users in food-insecure environments.

The BM-3 is primarily marketed to Tier 2 and Tier 3 augmented citizens who rely on high-draw cortical or sensory implants but lack the infrastructure access or financial means to maintain dedicated external charging hardware. In practice, it has also become popular among operatives and couriers in professions where going off-grid for extended periods is operationally necessary — the implant generates no detectable electromagnetic signature during normal operation, making it attractive for those who need to disappear from power-grid monitoring systems.
tier availability Tier 2+
developers
  • ZHENG-DAO BIOELECTRIC
  • NOVAFOLD PHARMACEUTICALS
  • HELIX BIOSYSTEMS
base technologies
  • Microbial fuel cell bioelectrochemistry
  • Exoelectrogenic bacterial engineering
  • Semi-permeable hydrogel membrane fabrication
  • Peritoneal implant surgical protocols
  • Rejection suppression pharmacology
enables
  • Off-grid neural augmentation operation
  • Extended covert operations without recharging infrastructure
  • High-draw cortical implant viability in low-infrastructure zones
  • Bioelectric power integration with implanted medical devices
social impact The CellStack BM-3 has created a troubling dependency loop for lower-tier augmented citizens: the implant solves the recharging problem but ties an individual's neural augmentation functionality directly to their nutritional status. In Meridian 88's food-insecure Tier 3 and 4 zones, users experiencing malnutrition report progressive degradation of augmentation performance — blurred neural interfaces, lag in prosthetic response, sensory implant dropout — before they experience obvious hunger symptoms. Zheng-Dao markets this as 'intuitive biofeedback,' but critics and street medics describe it as effectively penalizing poverty with cognitive impairment. The company's replacement chamber service contracts are priced to ensure ongoing revenue dependency, and colony viability voids are not covered under standard Meridian 88 augmentation warranties.
story hooks
  • A series of neural augmentation failures in the Cairn District traces back not to hardware defects but to a contaminated food supply deliberately depleted of the specific metabolites the BM-3 bacteria require — someone is selectively disabling augmented residents
  • Zheng-Dao's proprietary GeoElectric-7 bacterial strain has been reverse-engineered and is being cultured in a black-market biolab, but the bootleg version produces a byproduct that slowly damages the SIL membrane — users are experiencing internal infections months after installation
  • An operative hired for a deep-cover long-duration infiltration discovers her BM-3 output is being remotely monitored by Zheng-Dao through an undisclosed telemetry channel — the company knows exactly where she is and what she's eating
  • A Tier 4 community health worker is quietly helping residents maintain their BM-3 colonies through precise nutritional supplementation, effectively keeping an entire neighborhood's augmentations functional for free — Zheng-Dao has taken notice

Zheng-Dao Bioelectric FluxSpine BX-7 Kinetic Harvest Implant#📋

name Zheng-Dao Bioelectric FluxSpine BX-7 Kinetic Harvest Implant
brand name Zheng
product name Zheng-Dao Bioelectric FluxSpine BX-7 Kinetic Harvest Implant
type technology
aliases
  • The Eel
  • Spine Tap
  • BX
  • Kineharvest
subcategory energy
description The FluxSpine BX-7 is a surgically-implanted piezoelectric energy harvesting system that extracts usable electrical power from the mechanical stress of the host's skeletal and muscular activity. A lattice of flexible piezoelectric film strips — approximately 0.3mm thick — is bonded to the periosteum of the spine, femurs, and tibiae during a four-hour implantation procedure. Each flex, step, and postural adjustment generates micro-currents that are rectified and stored in a subcutaneous lithium-ceramic buffer cell seated near the iliac crest. Under normal ambulatory conditions, the BX-7 generates between 18 and 34 milliwatts continuously — insufficient for power-hungry augmentations alone, but enough to meaningfully extend the operational life of neural interfaces, retinal overlays, and embedded comms gear.

Zheng-Dao's key innovation in the BX-7 over earlier kinetic harvest systems is the BioSync adaptive film calibration protocol, which uses the implant's onboard microprocessor to continuously remap which film segments are generating optimal yield based on the host's gait, posture, and activity patterns. Over approximately six weeks post-implantation, the system effectively personalizes itself to the individual, improving output by an average of 40% compared to factory defaults. The buffer cell charges conventional augmentation power sockets via a subcutaneous induction pad, eliminating the need for the host to consciously manage charging cycles.

The BX-7 is standard equipment for Tier 3 and Tier 4 citizens who carry augmentations but cannot reliably afford or access charging infrastructure. It's also heavily used in the courier, dockworker, and construction labor sectors, where high daily step counts translate to meaningful energy yields. A significant gray market exists for BX-7 units harvested from deceased donors, which carry the previous host's calibration data and require painful re-calibration procedures.
tier availability Tier 3+
developers
  • ZHENG-DAO BIOELECTRIC
  • HELIX BIOSYSTEMS
base technologies
  • piezoelectric film deposition
  • subcutaneous lithium-ceramic buffer cells
  • inductive subcutaneous power transfer
  • adaptive microprocessor calibration
  • periosteal bonding techniques
enables
  • augmentation independence from external charging infrastructure
  • extended field operation for implanted devices
  • labor-class augmentation adoption
  • kinetic energy trading via body-worn mesh nodes
  • next-generation multi-source hybrid power implants
social impact The FluxSpine BX-7 has become a quiet symbol of class stratification in Meridian 88 — Tier 1 and 2 citizens charge their augmentations in dedicated suites or via building infrastructure, while lower-tier workers literally power their implants with the labor of their bodies. Employers in the logistics and construction sectors have begun factoring BX-7 output into augmentation benefit packages, framing it as worker empowerment while critics note it simply offloads infrastructure costs onto employees' skeletons. The gray market for cadaver-harvested units has driven a disturbing secondary economy around bodies recovered from gang territories and unclaimed morgue overflow.
story hooks
  • A labor organizer is building a case that Kelpline Logistics' policy of requiring BX-7 implants as a condition of employment constitutes corporate seizure of workers' biological output — and someone at Zheng-Dao wants the case buried
  • A BX-7 unit harvested from a murdered corporate courier still contains calibration data that, when decoded, maps the precise daily movement routes the courier walked — routes that lead somewhere no courier should have been

Zheng-Dao Bioelectric NerveBridge SX-11 Peripheral Nerve Regeneration Conduit#📋

name Zheng-Dao Bioelectric NerveBridge SX-11 Peripheral Nerve Regeneration Conduit
brand name Zheng
product name Zheng-Dao Bioelectric NerveBridge SX-11 Peripheral Nerve Regeneration Conduit
type technology
aliases
  • SilkWire
  • NeuroThread
  • The Bridge
  • SX-11
subcategory medical
description The NerveBridge SX-11 is a biodegradable electroactive conduit system developed by Zheng-Dao Bioelectric for repairing severed or severely damaged peripheral nerves in patients with traumatic limb injuries or augmentation-related nerve shear. The conduit itself is a 2–40cm tube constructed from a piezoelectric silk-fibroin matrix seeded with Schwann cell analogs — lab-grown support cells that guide regenerating axons across the gap site. A micro-embedded electrode array along the conduit's inner surface delivers pulsed low-frequency electrical stimulation autonomously, drawing power from the body's own bioelectric field through Zheng-Dao's proprietary ambient harvest circuitry, eliminating the need for external power sources or battery replacement.

The SX-11 represents the third major revision of Zheng-Dao's nerve conduit line, incorporating lessons from the SX-9's widely reported fibrotic encapsulation failures. The silk-fibroin matrix degrades cleanly over 8–12 months as the regenerating nerve matures, leaving no permanent foreign body. Critically, the SX-11 includes a wireless telemetry node that broadcasts nerve conduction velocity data to the attending physician's interface, allowing real-time monitoring of regeneration progress without invasive follow-up procedures. It is available in 23 standardized diameter configurations to match nerve gauges from fine digital branches to major peripheral trunks like the ulnar and sciatic.

The SX-11 occupies a peculiar market niche: it is primarily prescribed by Zheng-Dao-affiliated clinics as a prerequisite procedure before installing their higher-margin neural augmentation hardware, ensuring nerve pathways are intact and optimized for implant integration. Critics within the medical community note that this creates a financial incentive to recommend NerveBridge procedures even in cases where natural regeneration might suffice, effectively making the conduit a gatekeeping product for augmentation access.

social impact The NerveBridge SX-11 has quietly restructured who can access motor augmentation in Meridian 88 — since Zheng-Dao-affiliated clinics control both the conduit and the downstream augment hardware, patients with nerve damage must enter Zheng-Dao's care ecosystem to qualify for implants, creating long-term loyalty (and debt) relationships. In communities where industrial accidents and gang violence produce high rates of peripheral nerve injury, the SX-11 has become simultaneously a genuine lifeline and a mechanism of corporate capture, with Zheng-Dao financial products bundled into the treatment plan at nearly every step.

  • A veteran with a Zheng-Dao NerveBridge discovers her conduit's degradation timeline was deliberately extended in a firmware update, keeping her locked into Zheng-Dao's monitoring subscription longer than medically necessary
  • An underground ripperdoc claims to have reverse-engineered the SX-11's electrical stimulation protocol and wants funding to produce a non-degradable permanent version — with unknown long-term consequences

Zheng-Dao Bioelectric OsteoMatrix Flex-3 Bone Regeneration Scaffold#📋

name Zheng-Dao Bioelectric OsteoMatrix Flex-3 Bone Regeneration Scaffold
brand name Zheng
product name Zheng-Dao Bioelectric OsteoMatrix Flex-3 Bone Regeneration Scaffold
type technology
aliases
  • BoneGrid
  • Flex-Three
  • The Lattice
  • ZD Scaffold
subcategory medical
description The OsteoMatrix Flex-3 is a bioelectric bone regeneration scaffold system consisting of a patient-customized porous titanium-ceramic lattice printed to match the geometry of the damaged skeletal region, embedded with Zheng-Dao's proprietary piezoelectric microfilaments. These filaments generate micro-electrical fields in response to mechanical loading — mimicking the natural bioelectric signals that govern osteoblast activity — and accelerate bone ingrowth into the scaffold structure at rates 3.2x faster than passive scaffold competitors. The Flex-3 designation refers to the third-generation flexible integration zones at the scaffold's margins, which use a graduated porosity gradient to prevent the stress-shielding fractures that plagued rigid-margin earlier models. The scaffold is designed to be fully resorbed by host bone tissue over 18-24 months, leaving no permanent implant material.

The manufacturing process requires a pre-procedure CT scan fed into Zheng-Dao's cloud fabrication system, which produces the customized scaffold within 6 hours using sintered ceramic-titanium powder beds. This tight turnaround has made the OsteoMatrix system viable for trauma applications, not just elective reconstructive surgery. The embedded piezoelectric network also serves a secondary diagnostic function: an external wand reader (the ZD OsteoScan RS-2) can interrogate the scaffold's electrical output pattern to assess bone ingrowth progress non-invasively, replacing the need for follow-up imaging in most cases.

The OsteoMatrix Flex-3 has found a significant secondary market in Meridian 88's augmentation-adjacent economy. Citizens who require skeletal reinforcement to support heavy cybernetic limb attachments — where the bone-implant interface is a chronic failure point — use the Flex-3 scaffold to pre-strengthen attachment sites before augmentation surgery. Zheng-Dao markets this application through licensed augmentation clinics but quietly ignores its use in unlicensed chop-shop pre-augmentation prep.

social impact The OsteoMatrix Flex-3 has quietly become a prerequisite technology for the heavy augmentation market in Meridian 88. As corporations push employees toward heavier, more capable cybernetic limb systems for industrial and security work, skeletal failure at attachment points has become a recognized occupational injury category. Workers in Tier 3 industrial contracts who receive employer-mandated augmentation without accompanying skeletal reinforcement — because the scaffold adds cost the employer won't absorb — face a documented pattern of interface fractures within 3-5 years, creating a cycle of medical debt and re-injury that keeps augmented industrial workers economically dependent on their corporate employers.
story hooks
  • A labor organizer in the Palladian Construction workforce has compiled records showing that the company's medical contractors systematically omit OsteoMatrix scaffolding from worker augmentation packages despite internal safety assessments recommending it — she needs someone to get those internal documents out before the next contract renewal
  • A counterfeiting ring has been producing OsteoMatrix Flex-3 scaffolds using incorrect piezoelectric filament specifications — the scaffolds look identical but generate fields that inhibit rather than promote bone growth, and the first wave of patients is beginning to present with catastrophic scaffold failures
  • Zheng-Dao's cloud fabrication system for the OsteoMatrix has been compromised — someone has been inserting microscopic structural weaknesses into scaffolds destined for specific patients, and the modifications are subtle enough that they won't manifest as failure for 18 months

Zheng-Dao Bioelectric PulseGuard CR-2 Cardiac Rhythm Stabilization Implant#📋

name Zheng-Dao Bioelectric PulseGuard CR-2 Cardiac Rhythm Stabilization Implant
brand name Zheng
product name Zheng-Dao Bioelectric PulseGuard CR-2 Cardiac Rhythm Stabilization Implant
type technology
aliases
  • The Ticker
  • CR-2
  • HeartLock
  • Zheng Pulse
subcategory medical
description The PulseGuard CR-2 is a subdermal cardiac rhythm management implant roughly the size of a large coin, seated beneath the left clavicle via a 15-minute outpatient procedure. It combines a next-generation adaptive pacemaker with a bioelectric defibrillation module capable of delivering a precisely calibrated shock to terminate ventricular fibrillation without the energy demands that made earlier implanted defibrillators bulky and short-lived. The CR-2 harvests a portion of its operating power from the body's own bioelectric gradient using Zheng-Dao's established microcurrent harvesting architecture, supplemented by a solid-state reserve cell with a projected 12-year lifespan under normal load.

What distinguishes the CR-2 from legacy cardiac implants is its real-time arrhythmia classification engine — a compact neuromorphic processing unit that continuously analyzes cardiac waveforms and distinguishes between benign rhythm variations (common in heavily augmented individuals whose implants introduce bioelectric interference) and genuinely dangerous arrhythmias requiring intervention. This dramatically reduces inappropriate shock delivery, which had been a leading cause of augmentation-related cardiac injury in Meridian 88's densely wired population. The device communicates with Saltmarsh Telecom's health monitoring infrastructure when in range, forwarding anonymized rhythm data to Zheng-Dao's cardiac analytics network.

The CR-2 is standard issue for any citizen receiving thoracic augmentation above a certain implant density threshold, and Tier 1 corporate employees frequently receive it as a proactive measure regardless of cardiac history. In lower tiers, access is more complicated — the device is theoretically available through subsidized Tier 3 health programs, but waiting lists run 18 months or longer, and counterfeit units using inferior capacitor arrays have caused several documented deaths by delayed defibrillation. A persistent rumor in security circles holds that the CR-2's Saltmarsh data uplink can be exploited to deliver a targeted cardiac event — a claim Zheng-Dao dismisses as technically impossible and Arcturus Defense Solutions has reportedly been investigating for three years.

The data uplink controversy has fed a growing anti-telemetry movement in the lower tiers, where 'going dark' — removing or blocking health monitoring implants — is simultaneously a political statement, a survival strategy against corporate surveillance, and a genuine medical risk. For the corporations, the CR-2 network represents one of the most intimate datasets they hold: the literal heartbeats of their workforce, in real time.

description The SynapseWeave NX-6 is a ultra-thin biocompatible neural mesh — roughly 40 microns thick — that is injected in a folded suspension state and self-deploys across the cortical surface over 72 hours, guided by Zheng-Dao's proprietary electrotrophic migration algorithm. Once seated, the mesh's 2.4 million electrode nodes interface with native cortical tissue, creating a dense bidirectional communication layer between organic neurons and external digital systems. Unlike earlier invasive drill-point implants, the NX-6 achieves full-surface coverage without cranial surgery, instead entering via a lumbar spinal injection and traveling the cerebrospinal fluid pathways to the brain. The NX-6 distinguishes itself from competitors through its adaptive impedance matching — the mesh continuously recalibrates each electrode node to compensate for glial scarring and neural plasticity changes, maintaining signal fidelity over years rather than degrading. It supports simultaneous sensory augmentation, memory indexing, and direct device control. The mesh also includes a passive bioelectric harvesting layer that siphons enough energy from neural firing patterns to maintain low-power operations without a dedicated battery. Primary users are Tier 2 and above corporate knowledge workers, Saltmarsh Telecom's licensed netrunner corps, and the wealthier end of the grey-market augmentation scene.

  • Orbital counterweight station construction
enables
  • Low-cost access to orbit
  • Bulk cargo transport to space
  • Orbital manufacturing capability
  • Off-world resource utilization
social impact The space elevator has given Zheng-Dao a monopoly on orbital access from Meridian 88's region, and they leverage this monopoly aggressively. Any entity that wants to access space — for manufacturing, resource exploitation, or simply to escape Earth's jurisdiction — must negotiate with Zheng-Dao. The elevator's ground anchor is one of the most heavily defended installations in Meridian 88, as its destruction would strand orbital assets and end space access for decades. The tether itself is a vulnerability — a clean cut at any point would cause catastrophic recoil.
story hooks
  • Intelligence suggests a faction is planning to sever the space elevator tether — the resulting recoil would whip thousands of kilometers of carbon nanotube across the surface like a planet-sized wire saw.
  • Zheng-Dao has been quietly transporting something down the elevator that they are not declaring on cargo manifests — the climber vehicles are arriving heavier than they should be, and the ground facility has increased security.

Zheng-Dao Heavy Industries Subterranean Transit Network#📋

name Zheng-Dao Heavy Industries Subterranean Transit Network
brand name Zheng
product name Zheng-Dao Heavy Industries Subterranean Transit Network
type technology
aliases
  • STN
  • Undergrid
  • Mole Rail
  • Deep Transit
subcategory transportation
description A network of high-speed transit tubes buried 200-500 meters beneath Meridian 88's surface, using magnetic levitation and vacuum sealing to propel passenger and cargo pods at speeds exceeding 1,000 km/h. The STN connects major corporate facilities, government installations, and upper-tier residential districts through a transportation system that bypasses surface congestion entirely. The tunnels were excavated by DEAES autonomous boring systems and lined with reinforced materials capable of withstanding the geological pressures at depth. The network is invisible from the surface — most Meridian 88 residents do not know it exists.
tier availability Tier 4+
developers
  • ZHENG-DAO HEAVY INDUSTRIES
  • STERLING-NAKAMURA
base technologies
  • Deep tunnel magnetic levitation
  • Vacuum-sealed transit tube engineering
  • Autonomous tunnel boring and lining
enables
  • Ultra-high-speed subterranean transit
  • Bypass of surface traffic and surveillance
  • Secure transport of personnel and materials
  • Hidden infrastructure connectivity
social impact The STN represents a literal underground layer of privilege — a transportation system that only the powerful know about and only the authorized can access. Its existence means that corporate executives and high-tier residents can move between facilities without ever appearing on surface surveillance, creating a shadow geography of movement that is invisible to the broader population. The STN has also enabled covert military logistics, with Zheng-Dao using the network to move weapons and personnel beneath city streets without detection.
story hooks
  • A player character discovers an STN access point in an unexpected location — a forgotten maintenance hatch in a Tier 1 basement that opens onto a high-speed transit tube carrying corporate executives directly beneath the city's poorest district.
  • Someone has introduced a contaminant into the STN's vacuum system that is being carried through the tube network — every station and pod is being exposed to an unknown substance.

Zheng-Dao Heavy Industries Zero-Point Energy Research Platform#📋

name Zheng-Dao Heavy Industries Zero-Point Energy Research Platform
brand name Zheng
product name Zheng-Dao Heavy Industries Zero-Point Energy Research Platform
type technology
aliases
  • ZPERP
  • Vacuum Tap
  • Zero Point
  • Zheng-Dao Infinity
subcategory energy
description An experimental physics research facility investigating the extraction of usable energy from quantum vacuum fluctuations — the fundamental energy inherent in empty space itself. The ZPERP has demonstrated energy extraction at laboratory scale, producing measurable power output from what appears to be nothing, but the process requires more energy to initiate than it produces in current configurations. Zheng-Dao's research roadmap projects net positive energy extraction within a decade, which would represent a literally infinite energy source. The physics community is divided on whether the research is groundbreaking or a measurement error.
tier availability Tier 5
developers
  • ZHENG-DAO HEAVY INDUSTRIES
base technologies
  • Quantum vacuum fluctuation measurement
  • Casimir effect energy extraction
  • Zero-point field manipulation
enables
  • Potential infinite energy extraction from vacuum
  • Physics paradigm shift in energy production
  • Post-scarcity energy economics
  • Unlimited power for all applications
social impact The ZPERP represents either the most important physics discovery in human history or an extremely expensive measurement error, and the distinction has multi-trillion-credit implications. If zero-point energy extraction becomes viable, every existing energy technology — fusion, solar, geothermal — becomes obsolete overnight. Ouroboros Energy, Zheng-Dao's energy competitor, has invested heavily in discrediting the research while simultaneously pursuing their own zero-point program in secret.
story hooks
  • The ZPERP has achieved net positive energy extraction in a single test run — the result has been classified immediately, and the research team has been isolated from outside contact.
  • A ZPERP experiment produced an unexpected side effect — a localized region of space where physical constants appear to be slightly different from normal, suggesting that manipulating vacuum energy alters the fabric of reality.
